    9 hours 
    
    ## Tour Summary
    
    ## Tour Highlights
    
    ## Journey Inclusions
    
    ## Tour Inclusions
    
    ## Tour Requirements

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
RAW BOOKING NOTES (source of truth)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Kia Ora!
    
    Thank you for booking with ExperienceKart, we’re really looking forward to hosting you.
    
    We hope you enjoy the experience as much as we do.
    Below are a few important details to ensure everything is well prepared for your experience.
    
    We kindly ask that you review this information prior to travel and keep your voucher accessible during your experience.
    
    Should you have any specific preferences or requirements, please feel free to let us know.
    If you have any questions at any time, please feel free to reach out, we’re here to help.
    
    # Departure Information
    This is a fully guided private tour. We can pick you from any hotel or an Airbnb location from Auckland. 
    
    Any changes to your pick-up location must be confirmed with us with at least 24 hours prior to your tour departure. Please call us or contact us by email to confirm. You will find our contact details in our confirmation email.
    
    # General Information
    - Children below 8 years old are required to travel in a child safety car seat. 
    - Children between 5-7 years old are also recommended to be in a booster seat for their safety.
    - Tours are suitable for people of all ages and average fitness. Our scenic walks are short and optional. Please let our guide know at check in if you have any fitness concerns.
    - The caves are accessible to those with reasonable mobility, as there are good handrails and paths.
    - There is no wheelchair access at the Waitomo Glowworm Cave.
    - Caves average at a temperature of 16-17 degrees Celsius year round.
    - Boat tour capacity is up to 23 people.
    - Souvenir photos are available for purchase after your tour.
    
    # What to bring
    - Comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions; warm layered clothes work best.
    - A comfortable closed toe footwear with a good grip.
    - Sunscreen / Sunglasses / Sun hat.
    - Please also bring a jersey or coat if it is cold.
    - Bring your camera/GoPro to click the best shots.
    - A light rain jacket (just in case)/ Warm clothing.
    - Essential personal items.
    
    #Tour Requirements
    - Please wear comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions, sun protection, jacket/warm layer, & any essential personal items.
    - Easy and good fitness level required.
    - You must be able to climb and descend stairs and walk on unpaved or uneven terrain.
    - This is a guided walking tour and sensible walking footwear is recommended.
    - No photography is permitted inside the Waitomo caves, and cameras and Go Pros cannot be taken on the tour.
    - No backpacks or large bags allowed in cave due to delicate formations.
    - Please do not touch the formation in the cave as it discoloured easily, please help us protect our cave.
    
    We look forward to having you aboard!
    
    Ngā mihi nui,
    
    ExperienceKart
    Phone: 0800 EXP KART | 0800 397 527 | +64 21 026 60993 (Outside NZ)
    Office Hours: 07:00am - 07:00pm (Mon - Sun)
    Email: bookings@experiencekart.co.nz
    Website: https://www.experiencekart.co.nz/

      The caves are accessible to those with reasonable mobility, as there are good handrails and paths.
      There is no wheelchair access at the Waitomo Glowworm Cave.
      Caves average at a temperature of 16-17 degrees Celsius year round.
      Boat tour capacity is up to 23 people.
      Please do not touch the formation in the cave as it discoloured easily, please help us protect our cave.
      Easy and good fitness level required.
      You must be able to climb and descend stairs and walk on unpaved or uneven terrain.

RAW DESCRIPTION (source of truth)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    duration: 45 minutes - 1.25 hours
    meeting_point: Blackbutt Forest Reserve, Shellharbour NSW 2529
    accessibility: For all ages. Pram and wheelchair accessible. A nightly, low-sensory session at 5:15pm. Please refer to the FAQs on our website for further detail on wheelchair accessibility.
    pricing: - **Adult - $17.50** *(ages 18+)*
    - **Child - $12.50** *(ages 3-17 – Children under the age of 15 must be accompanied by an adult)*
    - **Family - $49.00** *(two adults and two children or one adult and three children)*
    - **Infant - free** *(ages 2 and under are free – Only available with an Adult/Family ticket)*
    description: #Immersive nighttime experience
    
    The Enchanted Forest returns with an all-new illuminated adventure – **“In Bloom.”**
    
    Step into a magical after-dark world where glowing florals, vibrant colour and cascading light transform the forest into a living, breathing garden.
    
    Be immersed in enchanting pathways, interactive installations, spectacular projections and custom soundscapes designed to bring nature to life in a completely new way.
    
    Follow the illuminated trails, discover hidden moments of wonder and experience the forest as it blossoms into a breathtaking spectacle of light, colour and imagination.
    what_is_included: **New highlights and returning crowd favourites include:**
    
    An all-new theme, “In Bloom,” where the forest transforms into a vibrant illuminated garden celebrating colour, nature and the beauty of the changing seasons
    
    - Spectacular large-scale floral light projections cascading across trees and natural landscapes
    
    - Glowing botanical installations and magical creatures hidden throughout the trail
    
    - Interactive pathways and projections that respond as guests move through the forest
    
    - Light-reactive installations that change colour and movement through touch and interaction
    
    - Dreamlike mist-filled environments illuminated with flowing colour, light and sound
    extras: 🚌 A complimentary shuttle bus runs every Friday and Saturday night between The Shellharbour Club and the event, starting at 5:45pm with two buses rotating. The last bus departs 10 minutes after the show ends. The Shellharbour Club offers convenient Grab & Go food and drinks for guests on the move.
    
    *Low-Sensory Sessions*
    One session each night at 6:00pm. Designed for people with specific sensory needs or who prefer a more subdued environment. These sessions feature smaller crowds and a lower-intensity light display due to the available ambient light.
    
    The low-sensory sessions provide an inclusive space for everyone to enjoy The Enchanted Forest. Don’t forget to bring along sunglasses, headphones, or your favourite sensory toy if you think it would be beneficial.
    faqs: - **How can I purchase tickets?**
    All ticket purchases are made online, from this website. We recommend purchasing your tickets well in advance so you don't miss out. Any tickets not purchased from this website are not valid.
    
    - **What happens if it's raining?**
    The Enchanted Forest is an outdoor event that operates in the event of rain or wind, so check the weather forecast and dress appropriately. Encouragingly, drizzle on a given night creates an additional medium upon which the lasers and lights reflect, providing guests with a unique experience.
    If the weather presents a safety risk, for example, an extreme weather event, we reserve the right to close The Enchanted Forest, to ensure the safety of our staff and guests. If we need to cancel certain sessions, an email and/or SMS will be sent to all ticket holders. Guests can reschedule their visit by following the directions in the supplied email.
    
    - **If I am unable to attend, can I reschedule the visit or be refunded?**
    Due to the limited number of sessions, we can not offer refunds. However, you can attend at another time provided that you reschedule at least 48 hours before your booked session time. You can reschedule your booking via the 'change booking' link in the ticket confirmation email. We want everyone to have the chance to experience this magical journey!

  [redo_desc_requirements]
      accessibility: For all ages. Pram and wheelchair accessible. A nightly, low-sensory session at 5:15pm. Please refer to the FAQs on our website for further detail on wheelchair accessibility.
      
      *Low-Sensory Sessions*
      One session each night at 6:00pm. Designed for people with specific sensory needs or who prefer a more subdued environment. These sessions feature smaller crowds and a lower-intensity light display due to the available ambient light.
  [redo_desc_cancellation]
      Due to the limited number of sessions, we can not offer refunds. However, you can attend at another time provided that you reschedule at least 48 hours before your booked session time. You can reschedule your booking via the 'change booking' link in the ticket confirmation email. We want everyone to have the chance to experience this magical journey!
      
      The Enchanted Forest is an outdoor event that operates in the event of rain or wind, so check the weather forecast and dress appropriately. Encouragingly, drizzle on a given night creates an additional medium upon which the lasers and lights reflect, providing guests with a unique experience.
      If the weather presents a safety risk, for example, an extreme weather event, we reserve the right to close The Enchanted Forest, to ensure the safety of our staff and guests. If we need to cancel certain sessions, an email and/or SMS will be sent to all ticket holders. Guests can reschedule their visit by following the directions in the supplied email.

      - **Child - $12.50** *(ages 3-17 – Children under the age of 15 must be accompanied by an adult)*
      - **Family - $49.00** *(two adults and two children or one adult and three children)*
      - **Infant - free** *(ages 2 and under are free – Only available with an Adult/Family ticket)*
      
      🚌 A complimentary shuttle bus runs every Friday and Saturday night between The Shellharbour Club and the event, starting at 5:45pm with two buses rotating. The last bus departs 10 minutes after the show ends. The Shellharbour Club offers convenient Grab & Go food and drinks for guests on the move.
      
      - **How can I purchase tickets?**
      All ticket purchases are made online, from this website. We recommend purchasing your tickets well in advance so you don't miss out. Any tickets not purchased from this website are not valid.
      
      - **What happens if it's raining?**
      The Enchanted Forest is an outdoor event that operates in the event of rain or wind, so check the weather forecast and dress appropriately. Encouragingly, drizzle on a given night creates an additional medium upon which the lasers and lights reflect, providing guests with a unique experience.
      If the weather presents a safety risk, for example, an extreme weather event, we reserve the right to close The Enchanted Forest, to ensure the safety of our staff and guests. If we need to cancel certain sessions, an email and/or SMS will be sent to all ticket holders. Guests can reschedule their visit by following the directions in the supplied email.

  [redo_desc_about]
      #Immersive nighttime experience
      
      The Enchanted Forest returns with an all-new illuminated adventure – “In Bloom.”
      
      Step into a magical after-dark world where glowing florals, vibrant colour and cascading light transform the forest into a living, breathing garden.
      
      Be immersed in enchanting pathways, interactive installations, spectacular projections and custom soundscapes designed to bring nature to life in a completely new way.
      
      Follow the illuminated trails, discover hidden moments of wonder and experience the forest as it blossoms into a breathtaking spectacle of light, colour and imagination.
  [redo_desc_what_included]
      New highlights and returning crowd favourites include:
      
      An all-new theme, “In Bloom,” where the forest transforms into a vibrant illuminated garden celebrating colour, nature and the beauty of the changing seasons
      
      - Spectacular large-scale floral light projections cascading across trees and natural landscapes
      
      - Glowing botanical installations and magical creatures hidden throughout the trail
      
      - Interactive pathways and projections that respond as guests move through the forest
      
      - Light-reactive installations that change colour and movement through touch and interaction
      
      - Dreamlike mist-filled environments illuminated with flowing colour, light and sound
  [redo_desc_what_excluded]
      🚌 A complimentary shuttle bus runs every Friday and Saturday night between The Shellharbour Club and the event, starting at 5:45pm with two buses rotating. The last bus departs 10 minutes after the show ends. The Shellharbour Club offers convenient Grab & Go food and drinks for guests on the move.
      
      Low-Sensory Sessions
      One session each night at 6:00pm. Designed for people with specific sensory needs or who prefer a more subdued environment. These sessions feature smaller crowds and a lower-intensity light display due to the available ambient light.
      
      The low-sensory sessions provide an inclusive space for everyone to enjoy The Enchanted Forest.
  [redo_desc_what_to_bring]
      Don’t forget to bring along sunglasses, headphones, or your favourite sensory toy if you think it would be beneficial.
  [redo_desc_duration_text]
      45 minutes - 1.25 hours
  [redo_desc_requirements]
      For all ages. Pram and wheelchair accessible. A nightly, low-sensory session at 5:15pm. Please refer to the FAQs on our website for further detail on wheelchair accessibility.
      
      Child - $12.50 (ages 3-17 – Children under the age of 15 must be accompanied by an adult)
      
      Infant - free (ages 2 and under are free – Only available with an Adult/Family ticket)
  [redo_desc_cancellation]
      What happens if it's raining?
      The Enchanted Forest is an outdoor event that operates in the event of rain or wind, so check the weather forecast and dress appropriately. Encouragingly, drizzle on a given night creates an additional medium upon which the lasers and lights reflect, providing guests with a unique experience.
      If the weather presents a safety risk, for example, an extreme weather event, we reserve the right to close The Enchanted Forest, to ensure the safety of our staff and guests. If we need to cancel certain sessions, an email and/or SMS will be sent to all ticket holders. Guests can reschedule their visit by following the directions in the supplied email.
      
      If I am unable to attend, can I reschedule the visit or be refunded?
      Due to the limited number of sessions, we can not offer refunds. However, you can attend at another time provided that you reschedule at least 48 hours before your booked session time. You can reschedule your booking via the 'change booking' link in the ticket confirmation email. We want everyone to have the chance to experience this magical journey!
  [redo_meeting_point]
      Blackbutt Forest Reserve, Shellharbour NSW 2529
  [redo_desc_other]
      - Adult - $17.50 (ages 18+)
      - Child - $12.50 (ages 3-17 – Children under the age of 15 must be accompanied by an adult)
      - Family - $49.00 (two adults and two children or one adult and three children)
      - Infant - free (ages 2 and under are free – Only available with an Adult/Family ticket)
      
      How can I purchase tickets?
      All ticket purchases are made online, from this website. We recommend purchasing your tickets well in advance so you don't miss out. Any tickets not purchased from this website are not valid.

    #Performance, Technology, Sophistication
    
    ##About
    Heir to the four-cylinder Brutale that created the naked supersport segment by setting new performance and style standards, this bike has already garnered outstanding market feedback, paving the way for a successful future. 
    Compact yet comfortable, essential yet bristling with unique details, light yet capable of breath-taking performance, the Brutale 800 RR offers the perfect platform for an RC model. 
    
    Model year 2018 
    
    ##More Information
    - Please select the number of days you would like to rent out the Scout for below
    - Please select any additional accessories needed for your trip
    
    **What model year are your bikes?**
    Our bikes are always current within the year and are updated to the new model year annually.
    
    **Do I need a motorcycle licence to rent?**
    Yes. All motorcycle drivers are required to have a motorcycle endorsement.
    
    **Do I need motorcycle insurance?**
    No. Riders will be covered under the program insurance given they sign the required waivers.
    
    **What riding experience do I need to ride a Motorcycle?**
    You should be an experienced rider and able to manage varying road and weather conditions. Depending on where you are from, be prepared to drive on the left side of the road. 
    
    **Optional:** bags with liners and windscreen
    - Km limit = 500 km per day (additional fees apply for additional kms)
    - Petrol = full on pick-up, full on return (if not filled on return – pump price plus admin fee) 
    
    ##What is included? 
    - Vehicle 
    - Insurance (excess) 
    - Roadside assistance 
    
    ##What do you need to bring? 
    - Driver’s licence 
    - Riding gear (Helmet – must be NZ approved / Jacket / Boots / Eye coverage)

    - Vehicle 
    - Roadside assistance 
    
    ##What do you need to bring? 
    - Driver’s licence or NZTA approved translation
    - Riding gear (Helmet – must be NZ approved / Jacket / Boots / Eye coverage)
    
    **Prior to arrival:**
    - Please complete the online waiver/form before you arrive 
    - Sign your rental contract

    **Key reminders:** 
    - Must have your motorcycle licence at the time of collection 
    - Must be paid in full and have contract signed 
    - Required riding gear to bring includes a NZ approved helmet 
    
    ##See you soon!

    #Hello yesterday - Easy Come, Easy Go
    
    ##About
    The 650 Twin is the rebirth of Royal Enfield’s legendary parallel twin cylinder engine. And it’s driving two Royal Enfield classic motorcycles – the Interceptor and the Continental GT. While classically styled and visually beautiful, the new engine is Royal Enfield’s most forward-looking yet, with a cleaner, elegant look, fewer components, less weight and easier maintenance. 
    
    Model year 2022
    
    ##More Information
    - Please select the number of days you would like to rent out the Scout for below
    - Please select any additional accessories needed for your trip
    
    **What model year are your bikes?**
    Our bikes are always current within the year and are updated to the new model year annually.
    
    **Do I need a motorcycle licence to rent?**
    Yes. All motorcycle drivers are required to have a motorcycle endorsement.
    
    **Do I need motorcycle insurance?**
    No. Riders will be covered under the program insurance given they sign the required waivers.
    
    **What riding experience do I need to ride a Motorcycle?**
    You should be an experienced rider and able to manage varying road and weather conditions. Depending on where you are from, be prepared to drive on the left side of the road. 
    
    **Optional:** bags with liners and windscreen
    - Km limit = 500 km per day (additional fees apply for additional kms)
    - Petrol = full on pick-up, full on return (if not filled un return – pump price plus admin fee)
    
    ##What is included? 
    - Vehicle 
    - Insurance (excess) 
    - Roadside assistance 
    
    ##What do you need to bring? 
    - Driver’s licence 
    - Riding gear (Helmet – must be NZ approved / Jacket / Boots / Eye coverage)

RAW BOOKING NOTES (source of truth)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Thankyou for booking Capricorn Caves Halloween Spooktacular - The Haunted Cave!!
    
    Be prepared for a spine-tingling Halloween experience like no other as you venture into the depths of the caves after dark with our special self-guided ‘Haunted’ Cave Tours. Who knows what mysteries you might encounter in the dimly lit underground world? Be prepared for anything… adults-only tours from 7:30pm (bring a spare pair of pants)!
    
    The bar will be open from 4pm and dinner will be served from 6.30pm
    
    ##Things to Know
    This Halloween we have incorporated a mouth-watering dinner to quench your tastebuds. If you didn't select your dinner option at the time of booking, or would like to make any changes please contact Capricorn Caves directly at reception@capricorncaves.com.au
    
    ##What to Wear
    Be sure to dress to impress this Halloween as we will have best-dressed prizes to give away! Please be mindful of the sensitive cave environment when making your costume to ensure all of your costume leaves the cave with you.
    
    Footwear is essential
    
    ##Things to Know
    - **Access:** The Cathedral Cave tour is easy walking with some stairs. 
    - **Lighting:** The Cathedral Cave tour is electrically lit with LED solar powered lights 
    - **Photography:** Photography permitted 
    - To protect the cave no smoking, eating or drinking is permitted on tour. (water excepted)

    ##Important hire notes: 
    
    Our equipment is not permitted to be taken offsite by vehicle. You must depart and return from our location on the equipment hired. 
    You must return on time to ensure you do not affect the next guest's rental. 
    
    ### Part day hires are not permitted to travel past Millpoint. 
    To visit the Figtree Point you must hire for a full day. 
    
    what_is_included: Three-seater Canoe
    Life Jackets
    Paddles

    - Complete your risk waiver
    - Receive safety instructions
    - Be issued your boarding pass
    
    🚫 Do not go directly to the beachfront, you’ll be sent back to reception to complete check-in.
    special_requirements: - Minimum age: 7 years
    - Children under 18 years must be accompanied by a parent or guardian
    - All participants must be able to swim
    - Risk waiver must be completed at check-in before hire
    restrictions: **Everglades Visits**
    Guests on a “Part Day Hire” (2.5 hours) are **NOT** permitted to visit the Everglades. You require a minimum of six hours to canoe round trip to the Noosa Everglade from our departure point. This means you must choose the full day canoe hire, and you must depart by 10am in order to make it back before the 4pm return time. It also requires a moderate level of fitness to Canoe for six hours.

      You must return on time to ensure you do not affect the next guest's rental. 
      
      ### Part day hires are not permitted to travel past Millpoint. 
      To visit the Figtree Point you must hire for a full day.
  [redo_desc_what_included]

  [redo_desc_requirements]

      - All participants must be able to swim
      - Risk waiver must be completed at check-in before hire
      
      **Everglades Visits**
      Guests on a “Part Day Hire” (2.5 hours) are **NOT** permitted to visit the Everglades. You require a minimum of six hours to canoe round trip to the Noosa Everglade from our departure point. This means you must choose the full day canoe hire, and you must depart by 10am in order to make it back before the 4pm return time. RAW DESCRIPTION (source of truth)

    duration: 1.5 Hours
    cancellation_summary: 24 Hours prior to the scheduled ride time. 
    meeting_point: Jarrahdale Equestrian Centre
    min_age: 10
    group_size: Valid for 2 riders
    description: Enjoy our 1.5 hour horse ride with a loved one and ride through the picturesque hills of Jarrahdale, and with this Christmas Offer receive 25% off our standard price! 
    
    See the beautiful native bush and quarry with some viewing lookouts over toward the Perth CBD, with cattle, sheep, kangaroos and an abundance of birds all around.
    
    No riding experience required. Medium level of fitness is required. Please note our weight limit is 110kg and minimum age on ride outs will be 10 years old. ** Surcharge applies for riders 6-9 years old - contact the office to book in**
    
    This is an exclusive adventure horse ride which means it is only the group you book to come and ride with you.This experience is truely catered and tailored for each individuals needs and abilities. All Ride outs are walking only.
    
    For groups larger than 2 or bookings within 48hrs please contact us. Office hours are Monday - Friday 9am - 5pm.
    
    Please note no dogs are permitted on the property at any time.
    what_is_included: - Trail guide
    - Horse/pony and tack
    - Helmet
    - On site parking 
    what_is_not_included: Please come dressed to ride, wearing:
    - Full length pants (jeans/leggings etc) 
    - Fully enclosed shoes with a slight heel and a smooth sole, sneakers will be suitable. 
    
    Please note singlets, shorts, dresses are NOT safe to ride in. If wearing incorrect attire you will not be permitted to ride and your ride will be cancelled, with no refund applicable.
    
    In Summer bring drinking water for during/after your ride, sunscreen and longer sleeves for sun protection. 
    highlights: Ride through the Hills and Farmland surrounding Jarrahdale Equestrian. 
    
    Spot wildlife and farmlife along the way to one of our lookouts with views toward the Perth CBD and Ocean. 
    check_in_details: You will need to register as a rider with us prior to your ride. If you do this online and fill out your insurance forms before you arrive it will save time on the day, otherwise make sure to give yourself additional time to complete this out here at the stables to avoid cutting into your ride time. https://jarrahdaleequestrian.com.au/register/
    
    special_requirements: Due to insurance requirements riders aged 6-9 are to be on a lead line for the duration of the ride, which may alter availability and with a surcharge applicable.
    
    Nervous riders can also request to have an additional instructor preset to lead line them / their horse for the duration of the ride, which may alter availability and with a surcharge applicable.
    
    restrictions: Horse Riding is considered a high risk activity therefore PREGNANT RIDERS WILL NOT BE PERMITTED TO RIDE, NO EXCEPTIONS and no refund offered.
    
    Rides using this offer must be completed on / before the 28th of February 2026.
    
    Use of additional discount or offer is not permitted on this offer. 
    disclaimers: We ride in all weather and please understand that all bookings are non refundable and we require 24hrs notice for rescheduling otherwise full fare is due.
    
    Our rides will start on time and be limited to the session you have booked so we recommend arriving 5-10 minutes early for all session. If arriving late your session will not be extended and you will be cutting into your ride time. No refund will be applicable for shorter sessions.

Please note our weight limit is 110kg and minimum age on ride outs will be 10 years old. ** Surcharge applies for riders 6-9 years old - contact the office to book in** This is an exclusive adventure horse ride which means it is only the group you book to come and ride with you. This experience is truly catered and tailored for each individual's needs and abilities. All Ride outs are walking only. For groups larger than 2 or bookings within 48hrs please contact us. Office hours are Monday - Friday 9am - 5pm. Please note no dogs are permitted on the property at any time.
  [redo_desc_highlights]
      Ride through the Hills and Farmland surrounding Jarrahdale Equestrian. Spot wildlife and farmlife along the way to one of our lookouts with views toward the Perth CBD and Ocean.
  [redo_desc_what_included]
      - Trail guide
      - Horse/pony and tack
      - Helmet
      - On site parking
  [redo_desc_what_excluded]
      Please come dressed to ride, wearing:
      - Full length pants (jeans/leggings etc)
      - Fully enclosed shoes with a slight heel and a smooth sole, sneakers will be suitable.
      
      Please note singlets, shorts, dresses are NOT safe to ride in. If wearing incorrect attire you will not be permitted to ride and your ride will be cancelled, with no refund applicable.
      
      In Summer bring drinking water for during/after your ride, sunscreen and longer sleeves for sun protection.
  [redo_desc_what_to_bring]

  [redo_desc_requirements]
      Due to insurance requirements riders aged 6-9 are to be on a lead line for the duration of the ride, which may alter availability and with a surcharge applicable.
      Nervous riders can also request to have an additional instructor preset to lead line them / their horse for the duration of the ride, which may alter availability and with a surcharge applicable.
      Horse Riding is considered a high risk activity therefore PREGNANT RIDERS WILL NOT BE PERMITTED TO RIDE, NO EXCEPTIONS and no refund offered.
  [redo_desc_cancellation]
      We ride in all weather and please understand that all bookings are non refundable and we require 24hrs notice for rescheduling otherwise full fare is due.
  [redo_desc_check_in]
      You will need to register as a rider with us prior to your ride. If you do this online and fill out your insurance forms before you arrive it will save time on the day, otherwise make sure to give yourself additional time to complete this out here at the stables to avoid cutting into your ride time. https://jarrahdaleequestrian.com.au/register/
  [redo_min_age]
      10
  [redo_group_size]
      Valid for 2 riders
  [redo_meeting_point]
      Jarrahdale Equestrian Centre
  [redo_desc_other]
      Use of additional discount or offer is not permitted on this offer. Rides using this offer must be completed on / before the 28th of February 2026. Our rides will start on time and be limited to the session you have booked so we recommend arriving 5-10 minutes early for all session. If arriving late your session will not be extended and you will be cutting into your ride time. No refund will be applicable for shorter sessions.

RAW DESCRIPTION (source of truth)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    ## Rates and Fees
    (Per Person)
    
    We require a 10-person minimum for Splatball to take place (See FAQ for more information). The total package amount is required when booking. All prices include GST.
    
    -
    
    **Unlimited Splatball Package – A$49.99**
    
    Includes:
    - Unlimited Splatballs during the games
    - Supply of Splatball gun, mask and camo overalls.
    - Safety briefing, professional instruction and site induction.
    - As much bottled water as you can drink and lots of FUN!
    ___________________________________
    ## Duration
    90 Minutes
    
     - This time frame will see about 6-8 games played in total on up to 2 different playing arenas.
     - Parents/Organizers welcome to watch during game play.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
RAW BOOKING NOTES (source of truth)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    ##More Information
    
    **Arrival:**Please arrive 15 minutes ahead of your booking time to complete registration.
    
    **Bring on the Day:**All players will need to wear enclosed shoes (sneakers or boots) and it is recommended to wear comfortable light clothing to go under the overalls on the day (t-shirt & shorts). If you are participating in Paintball, you will need to bring your ID on the day.
    
    **Other**All players must have a 0.00 blood alcohol level to operate the paintball gun and not be under the influence of any drugs. 
    
    Click [here](https://www.phoenixpaintball.com.au/faq/) to see our FAQ page
    
    **Field Address:**11 Depot Road, Rawson, Victoria , 3825
    
    **Directions:** From Melbourne (allow 2-2½ hours) West Gippsland (allow 1 hour from Warragul) take the Princes Highway (M1) to Moe then C466 through Erica. From Sale and Traralgon take C481 through Tyers. The property entrance is off Depot Rd, first driveway on the left approx. 20m down the road.
    
    We are actually on the property of "MOUNTAIN RIVERS TOURIST PARK" There is a Phoenix Paintball sign at the driveway entrance. Drive past the Office and Lodge to the Paintball (lower) car park area which you will see on the left hand side at the end of the driveway.
    
    For Latrobe Valley and East Gippsland patrons, it is a short 25-30 minute drive from Moe or Traralgon.

PRODUCT 402575   (524 words)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
RAW DESCRIPTION (source of truth)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    meeting_point: Urimbirra Wildlife Park
    description: ###About
    
    A morning group aiming to enjoy and interact with like-minded parents to develop and expose their young children (5 years and under) to nature and our environment. Starting with a shared morning tea and Story time - we explore some of our great Australian Authors, and then continuing on with a guided walk around the perimeter of the Park, meeting and feeding our very friendly inhabitants. We also play games, sing songs and generally celebrate our wildlife through play!. Fantastic physical exercise - while letting the benefits of the great outdoors seep into our skin! 
    You are most welcomed to stay longer in the Park after the session.
    Price includes 1 Adult and 1 child. 
     Please bring water and snacks to share.
    Extra children; 
    3 and under are free. 
    4 and over are $5 per child. 5 year olds transitioning into school are also welcome 
    Extra Adult $15 per adult.
    
    >####Call Mandy on 0413 799 599 to ask about last-minute bookings!

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
RAW BOOKING NOTES (source of truth)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    #Cost includes entry for one adult and 1 child. Extra children 4-years and over can be added $5 per child No charge for children 3 and under. Extra adult $15
    Please bring a preferred snack and water for your child/children which caters to their dietary requirements.
    
    Thanks for joining our Wildlife Babies Group! Please ensure you and your child are wearing comfortable clothes - that are weather appropriate - and covered in shoes (boots or sneakers) that are able to get dirty! You will need to bring a hat, water, sunscreen and food for their morning tea. They usually build up an appetite! Looking forward to meeting you all, I am excited to be able to give you an insight into our wonderful, unique Wildlife. 
    
    Product Terms and Conditions
    .
    The Wildlife Babies Program will only run when a minimum of 2 participants are booked on a particular date. In the event that there is only one participant booked, you will be contacted prior to the day and given the opportunity to select an alternative date.
    
    You and your Children will be participating in feeding the animals, which may cause injury. Urimbirra Wildlife Park and the Program Coordinator takes every effort to ensure the safety of you and your child, however do not accept responsibility for injuries that may occur.
    
    Photographs/Video may be taken during the day for publicity purposes. Please indicate if you wish this NOT to happen
    
    The Park has a hot and cold weather policy (If the weather is forecast over 35 degrees or excessive rain) should this affect your booking; we will contact you to make alternative arrangements.
    
    On Catastrophic Fire Days the event will be cancelled.
    
    We require at least 36 hours notice of rescheduling 
    
    If less than 24 hours notice of cancellation is given we will retain a non- refundable amount of $20.00 as booking fee, the remainder will be refunded. This will allow the program to go ahead if the number falls below 2 participants.
    
    Please Bring
    
    • Comfortable well fitting clothes – that are able to get dirty
    
    • Covered in shoes/hat
    
    • Water bottle/snacks
    
    • Camera/sunscreen id required

      The Wildlife Babies Program will only run when a minimum of 2 participants are booked on a particular date. In the event that there is only one participant booked, you will be contacted prior to the day and given the opportunity to select an alternative date.
      You and your Children will be participating in feeding the animals, which may cause injury. Urimbirra Wildlife Park and the Program Coordinator takes every effort to ensure the safety of you and your child, however do not accept responsibility for injuries that may occur.
      Photographs/Video may be taken during the day for publicity purposes. Please indicate if you wish this NOT to happen
      The Park has a hot and cold weather policy (If the weather is forecast over 35 degrees or excessive rain) should this affect your booking; we will contact you to make alternative arrangements.
      On Catastrophic Fire Days the event will be cancelled.
      We require at least 36 hours notice of rescheduling
      If less than 24 hours notice of cancellation is given we will retain a non- refundable amount of $20.00 as booking fee, the remainder will be refunded. This will allow the program to go ahead if the number falls below 2 participants.
  [redo_booking_other]
      Thanks for joining our Wildlife Babies Group! Please ensure you and your child are wearing comfortable clothes - that are weather appropriate - and covered in shoes (boots or sneakers) that are able to get dirty! You will need to bring a hat, water, sunscreen and food for their morning tea. They usually build up an appetite! Looking forward to meeting you all, I am excited to be able to give you an insight into our wonderful, unique Wildlife.

  [redo_desc_about]
      A morning group aiming to enjoy and interact with like-minded parents to develop and expose their young children (5 years and under) to nature and our environment. Starting with a shared morning tea and Story time - we explore some of our great Australian Authors, and then continuing on with a guided walk around the perimeter of the Park, meeting and feeding our very friendly inhabitants. We also play games, sing songs and generally celebrate our wildlife through play!. Fantastic physical exercise - while letting the benefits of the great outdoors seep into our skin! 
      You are most welcomed to stay longer in the Park after the session.
